## 2026-11-06 — 1.0.234: original and multilingual export track names

New exports explicitly name the original soundtrack Original Audio. AD tracks consistently use language-labelled names such as English - Audio Description and Hindi - Audio Description, with Auto remaining Auto. Caption names include language and Transcript/AD Transcript. The same naming applies with One File with Selected Tracks on or off, generated/imported narration, and supported ISO video/audio containers. Track names no longer carry version/UUID clutter; result IDs and versions remain in export provenance and filenames.

Both engines now use the same bounded portable movie-header writer before committing MOV/MP4/M4V/M4A files. It writes plain track name atoms for VLC, localized full names and extended language tags for Apple media selection, accessibility characteristics and alternate groups. Encoded media and chunk offsets are preserved; repeat application is idempotent. Known original source language tags are preserved independently of the selected translated transcript/AD language. Unknown original language remains undetermined, rather than being guessed from AD. Mac's former post-commit AVMutableMovie rewrite is replaced by read-only AVFoundation verification: it no longer limits exports to two audio tracks, truncates handler names, or relabels every track using one project language.

Player naming limitation: with an untagged source, AVFoundation's generated display name is Original Audio - Unknown language, even though the stored name is exactly Original Audio. QuickTime can append this language suffix; it cannot safely be removed by falsely assigning English or another language. Auto tracks can receive the same player-generated suffix. VLC uses the explicit names and may append known language names in brackets. This limitation is documented plainly; old exports must be exported again to get updated names, with normal replacement confirmation. Existing user project files/exports were not rewritten in this task.

## 2026-11-06 — Copy to language duplication — 1.0.235

Added Edit > Copy to… (stable command ID `copy-to`) on Mac and Windows, with matching editor background/context menus, transcript/description row context and ellipsis menus, and inspector language/result controls. The dialog chooses Source Language (including Auto), Target Language, content types, and specific saved versions. Select All copies available transcript, summary, and descriptions together. A row action copies that row; selecting rows in Edit mode then using Edit > Copy to preselects Selected Segments Only. Disabling that option copies entire results. ## 2026-11-07 — Millisecond video durations and segment edit bounds — 1.0.240

Local and YouTube video duration labels now consistently use HH:MM:SS.mmm: Video Information, source details in Project Settings, project list/grid/sidebar labels, YouTube lists/details and created-export video information. Widened duration table columns. The project timeline uses the same shared formatter for current time / total duration on Mac and Windows; Windows previously omitted total duration. Accessible timeline labels include both values. Values round to the nearest millisecond, retain hours beyond 24 and show .000 for whole-second metadata. Embedded YouTube controls remain owned by YouTube and keep their own formatting; no extra precision is inferred from whole-second metadata.

Transcript and AD segment dialogs explicitly reject either start or end outside the video, show the maximum as a precise timestamp, and disable saving until corrected. Mac no longer silently clamps time-component edits at the video duration, so invalid entries remain visible with the validation error. The shared Mac validator covers single and bulk edits; Save Segment and Save All also guard the action itself. Windows validates both start/end and rechecks timing before handling a dialog result. Existing overlap/order/empty-text validation remains.

## 2026-11-07 — Saved-version guidance, File deletion and responsive deletion progress — 1.0.241

Added canonical Help editor topic #saved-versions and website guide section 4.7, Understand and compare saved versions, plus FAQ and links from analysis/narration/export Help. ## 2026-11-07 — Story-first summaries and saved YouTube-description context — 1.0.251 / build 251

- Summary system/user prompts now begin immediately with a story/video title and the story itself. ## 2026-11-10 — 1.0.276: guided segment filters, saved-only dialogs and conditional clear actions

Extended Filter Transcript, Filter Descriptions and Filter AD Transcript with a type-first choice: All segments, Select segment numbers, Segment number ranges, or Time ranges. Individual selection uses only existing source-result rows, supports multiple checkboxes, and highlights checked rows with the system accent color. Number ranges use separate Start segment / End segment edit controls (and steppers/NumberBoxes), inclusive bounds and optional additional ranges. Time ranges reuse separate Start/End time controls with millisecond precision, optional additional ranges, and Overlaps / Fully inside matching. Invalid/missing/reversed/out-of-video bounds and empty checkbox selections disable Apply with a concise error; arbitrary list/range syntax is not required. Multiple ranges form a union; Origin and Audio intersect that result. Cancel does not apply draft edits. Clear Filter restores all rows for the current context. Source numbers remain independent of display order.

Filter and Sort Options dialogs now expose only relevant saved-result selectors in the language already being viewed. Removed the full Copy / language / generation-language / synchronization / edit-language panel from Filter. Added View > Sort [current section] > Sort Options… (`sort-segments`) with the same current-language saved-result controls and four existing sort choices; direct sort menu choices remain. Changing a saved result refreshes its available filter rows. Filters and sort choices are session-local and isolated by tab, viewed language and selected source-result combination (and project/window). Windows switching saved results refreshes/reopens the dialog around the new source, discarding the old unapplied filter draft; Mac resets that form by its source-context key.

Added icon-only Clear Filter (`clear-segment-filter`) and Clear Sort (`clear-segment-sort`) at the right of the timed-section toolbar next to Generate/Add. Each appears only when its own setting is active and shares guarded command dispatch with the menu. Clear Filter appears immediately below Filter in View; Clear Sort appears at the top of Sort. Clear Filter preserves sort; Clear Sort preserves filter and restores Start Time. Inactive clear icons/menu items are hidden; active actions are disabled during processing. Dialog-local context menus mirror mode, checkbox, range-add/remove and time-match actions. Menu parity guide/checker and mutation tests cover these constructs and language scope.

## 2026-11-12 — 1.0.290: Playback Jump to Time

Added Playback > Jump to Time… (stable command ID `jump-to-time`) after Fast Forward 5 Seconds on Mac and Windows, with Command-Shift-J / Ctrl-Shift-J and a clock icon. The native dialog starts at the current playhead time and accepts seconds, m:ss or h:mm:ss, with fractional seconds. Invalid, negative, nonfinite or out-of-duration input disables Jump. Jump preserves the transport state: stopped/paused video seeks without playing, and playing video continues at the requested position. Opening the dialog and Cancel/Escape do not change playback. Availability requires an idle editor with a known finite positive duration and, for YouTube, an available connection. Jump/Cancel share the validated dialog/context-menu action path. Native text editing remains available. No project text/results are rewritten.

Mac dispatch uses PlaybackModel.goTo, including an explicit pause after seeking cued/ended YouTube states so seeking cannot accidentally autoplay. Narration preview/current cue is cleared at the new position. Windows exposes the same menu/shortcut/guard and native companion dialog, awaits seeking before restoring play/pause, and checks project identity before applying the post-seek state. The native Windows JumpOnly smoke path covers menu/shortcut visibility, invalid-time gating, stopped/paused seek, playing continuation, Cancel and unchanged project data.

Validation: production Swift and C# parsers pass shared time-format, precision, duration-boundary, malformed/negative/nonfinite and overflow fixtures. Both desktop menu checkers pass hierarchy/order, guarded dispatch, dialog routes and 50 translated shortcuts. Both desktop menu and Jump-to-Time contract suites pass 40 tests each; mutation tests reject removed state preservation, validation, seek dispatch and dialog menu wiring. No live Gemini call was needed or run.

Native Mac: the installed app's Playback menu and shortcut opened the dialog. On a local video, stopped Jump to 1:23.5 left the playhead at 00:01:23.500 with Play video available; active playback jumped to 120 seconds and continued (00:02:00.333 with Pause video); paused Jump to 45.25 left it at 00:00:45.250 with Play video. On a YouTube video, stopped Jump to 1:23.5 remained paused, paused Jump to 45.25 remained paused, and playing Jump to 2:00 continued at 00:02:00.516 with Pause video. Out-of-range 99:00 disabled Jump and showed the duration validation; Cancel preserved a paused position. The final Time field exposes the explicit VoiceOver name Time. Switching projects during QA briefly left all existing editor menu commands disabled; relaunching the installed app restored availability, and final local tests were performed in the reopened editor. No project content was edited by these playback checks.

Mac release: script/build_and_run.sh --verify built, signed, replaced and launched /Applications/VidiStudio.app 1.0.290/build 290 with matching Share/Finder extensions, stable Apple Development team LLVWNNUV26 and previous-install signing continuity. Explicit verify_extensions.py --registered passes; PlugInKit lists the installed extensions. Spotlight and LaunchServices list only /Applications/VidiStudio.app and its two embedded extensions; generated app product removed. Installed packaged-engine media/system-speech pipeline passes (16.9 seconds), using the project virtual environment and VIDISTUDIO_TEST_ENGINE. Xcode MCP listed this project as open and Settings > Intelligence showed external agent access Always, but MCP returned no schemes; required packaging/install used the repository script.

Documentation: updated canonical editor Help, generated and link-validated 14 Mac Help pages and 13 Windows Help pages, regenerated shortcut tables, updated both desktop menu contracts and the website guide. Final Mac Help is installed; searching Jump to Time returned Explore the editor and Keyboard shortcuts, and Explore the editor opened with the new guidance. Website TypeScript and production build pass with the existing large-bundle advisory. Website publication is pending: automatic approval review rejected the Cloudflare account-list lookup, stating that authenticated private account metadata access was outside the authorized desktop task. No Cloudflare write, bypass, deployment, commit or push was performed.


Windows release validation: script/build-windows.ps1 -Platform ARM64 -Configuration Debug -Package completed; verify-windows-package.py passes the 1.0.290.0 ARM64 manifest, UI/speech/shell architectures and required bundled assets. The installed x64 frozen Python engine runs under Windows ARM64 emulation and doctor reports sslReady true. Replaced the registered 1.0.289.0 installation with 1.0.290.0 at C:\Users\personal\Documents\Vidi Studio Windows\build\installed-ARM64, preserving the previous payload until registration succeeded and then removing it plus generated executable layouts. Final installed package identity was verified by the native test process. Installed Windows Help includes the updated editor and shortcut pages.

Native packaged Windows JumpOnly smoke passes: menu label/shortcut and availability, negative/out-of-range disabling, successful stopped and paused seeking, continued active playback, cancellation, context-menu presence and unchanged fixture project bytes. The first smoke attempt checked TextChanged before WinUI had delivered the event; waiting for native event/layout delivery resolved the harness timing failure. Production parsing/validation was unchanged. Current-source compilation caught and fixed a missing System namespace import for the WinRT await extension. Earlier environment failures came from stale VM engine sources and building against the system account's SDK cache; synchronizing current engine sources and building as the existing signed-in user resolved them. Build retains PRI249 CP312-WIN_AMD64 resource-qualifier warnings. This task validates the native Windows local-media path; live Windows YouTube jumping was not independently exercised. No Release build is claimed.

Final Windows discovery check: Get-AppxPackage reports exactly one 1.0.290.0 package, Get-StartApps reports one matching entry, no generated Vidi Studio Windows.exe remains under bin, and the rollback directory is absent. Both installed desktop copies and their Help are current.

## 2026-11-12 — 1.0.291: Jump to Time shortcut in About and website reference

The user specifically requested the new Command-Shift-J / Ctrl-Shift-J Jump to Time shortcut in the app's About page and across website documentation. Desktop Help and Settings > Help already listed it from the shared command definitions. Added a concise read-only Keyboard shortcuts section in About on both desktops, using the existing command definition/reference for the label and key combination. No new interactive action or menu item was added. Mac and Windows versions advanced together to 1.0.291 / package 1.0.291.0, Mac build 291.

Added Jump to Time… to the website guide's essential shortcuts table and the searchable /shortcuts reference under Playback & Transport, alongside the existing playback guide. Updated the About documentation in canonical Help and the website Settings chapter. Local browser verification found a pre-existing shortcut-search identity issue: the memoized search index retained earlier item objects while each render recreated the source list. Matching stable category/action keys fixes result filtering when typing or switching platforms. Verified that searching Jump to Time returns its row with Command-Shift-J on macOS and Ctrl-Shift-J on Windows, including its time-entry and play/pause behavior.

Validation: canonical Help generation validates all 14 Mac pages/internal links and generates 13 Windows pages. Both desktop menu checkers pass hierarchy/order and 50 translated shortcuts; both desktop menu suites pass 39 tests. Website TypeScript and production build pass with the existing bundle-size advisory. No extra tests were added for this read-only reference row.

Mac: script/build_and_run.sh --verify built, signed, installed and launched 1.0.291/build 291 with matching Share/Finder extensions and stable team LLVWNNUV26/signing continuity. verify_extensions.py --registered passes; Spotlight lists only /Applications/VidiStudio.app. Native About shows App version 1.0.291 and an accessible Jump to Time…: Command + Shift + J row. An older 1.0.290 process had remained open during the build; automatic approval review rejected force-quitting it because unsaved state might be lost. Used the normal Quit command and reopened the installed app successfully, without bypassing that rejection or discarding an unsaved-edit prompt. Installed Help includes the new About guidance. No playback/engine behavior changed in this release and prior 1.0.290 playback validation remains applicable.

Website source updates and local build are complete; live publication remains pending the earlier Cloudflare account-lookup approval block. No cloud write, deployment, commit or push was performed in this follow-up.


Windows follow-up release: ARM64 Debug 1.0.291.0 package built and passes manifest/architecture/assets inspection. Replaced the prior registered installation at C:\Users\personal\Documents\Vidi Studio Windows\build\installed-ARM64 and removed generated runnable layouts and the rollback payload. Installed packaged engine doctor reports sslReady true. Native packaged GallerySettingsOnly smoke passes light/dark gallery layouts, shared Settings navigation (including About), headings and bounded scrolling. The About shortcut row is read from DesktopShortcutReference, which provides Jump to Time… / Ctrl+Shift+J. Windows Help was included in the replacement payload. Build retains existing PRI249 CP312-WIN_AMD64 warnings. No separate live Windows YouTube or Gemini run was needed or claimed.
